## `AFG-9103-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Mycorrhizal-Fungi-PCR Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Endangered Species Cryopreserved Semen & Artificial Seawater Salinity
- **Intro:** Mycorrhizal-Fungi-PCR Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Endangered Species Cryopreserved Semen & Artificial Seawater Salinity — Pre-Operational Verification and PPE Compliance: LN2 dewar pressure: 1.5 bar ± 0.1 bar; ambient temperature: 20°C ± 2°C; relative humidity: 40-60% RH.
